  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/pipeline/collect_v4.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"V4 collect: bf16 direct-to-local, post-EOS extension, save assistant_mask + document_ids.\n\nDifferences vs collect_async.py:\n1. Reads JSONL from build_prompts_v4 with input_ids (already tokenized),\n   sends to /v1/completions as `prompt=input_ids` (not text)\n2. max_tokens=50, ignore_eos=True → target self-extends past <|im_end|> for\n   50 tokens, hook captures aux_hidden over the extension naturally\n3. bf16 direct-write to local data dir, NO NVFP4, NO BOS upload\n4. Save assistant_mask + document_ids alongside aux_hidden in each .pt\n5. assistant_mask for the post-EOS extension = True (we want loss on it)\n6. Pre-allocate KV cache budget per shard length so server doesn't OOM\n\nRun:\n    # 1. Start sglang server with EAGLE3 hook (see eval/start_collect.sh)\n    # 2. Then:\n    python3 eagle/pipeline/collect_v4.py \\\\\n        --in /tmp/eagle3_prompts_v4_smoke.jsonl \\\\\n        --out_dir /user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/data/train_v4\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport signal\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport aiohttp\nimport torch\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nAPI_BASE       = \"http://127.0.0.1:30000\"\nCONCURRENCY    = 16    # lower than v2's 32 because samples can be 64K\nBATCH_SIZE     = 32    # smaller batch since long shards\nFLUSH_WAIT_SEC = 0.5\nREQ_TIMEOUT_SEC = 1800\n\nPOST_EOS_TOKENS = 50    # ignore_eos extension length\n\n\nasync def check_server(session):\n    try:\n        async with session.get(f\"{API_BASE}/v1/models\",\n                               timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=10)) as r:\n            return r.status == 200\n    except Exception:\n        return False\n\n\n_FAIL = defaultdict(int)\n\n\nasync def send_one(session, sample, sem):\n    async with sem:\n        try:\n            async with session.post(\n                f\"{API_BASE}/v1/completions\",\n                json={\n                    \"model\": \"default\",\n                    \"prompt\": sample[\"input_ids\"],   # send token ids directly\n                    \"max_tokens\": POST_EOS_TOKENS,\n                    \"temperature\": 0,\n                    \"ignore_eos\": True,              # force continuation past <|im_end|>\n                },\n                timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=REQ_TIMEOUT_SEC),\n            ) as resp:\n                if resp.status != 200:\n                    body = (await resp.text())[:120]\n                    _FAIL[f\"http_{resp.status}\"] += 1\n                    return None, body\n                body = await resp.json()\n                if \"error\" in body or body.get(\"object\") != \"text_completion\":\n                    _FAIL[f\"body_err\"] += 1\n                    return None, str(body)[:120]\n                # extract generated tokens (the 50-tok extension)\n                choice = body[\"choices\"][0]\n                ext_text = choice.get(\"text\", \"\")\n                # Server may not return token_ids — we'll have hook output\n                return ext_text, None\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n            _FAIL[\"timeout\"] += 1\n            return None, \"timeout\"\n        except Exception as e:\n            _FAIL[f\"other:{type(e).__name__}\"] += 1\n            return None, str(e)[:60]\n\n\nasync def main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--in\", dest=\"in_path\", default=\"/tmp/eagle3_prompts_v4_smoke.jsonl\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--out_dir\", default=\"/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/data/train_v4\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--collect_dir\", default=\"/tmp/eagle3_collect_v4\",\n                    help=\"hook output dir; cleared per batch\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--limit\", type=int, default=0, help=\"0 = all samples\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir)\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    collect_dir = Path(args.collect_dir)\n    collect_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Load samples\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] reading {args.in_path}\")\n    samples = []\n    with open(args.in_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            samples.append(json.loads(line))\n    if args.limit > 0:\n        samples = samples[:args.limit]\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] loaded {len(samples)} samples\")\n\n    # Clear stale .pt\n    for p in collect_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"):\n        p.unlink()\n\n    sender_pbar = tqdm(total=len(samples), desc=\"send\", unit=\"req\")\n    save_pbar   = tqdm(total=len(samples), desc=\"save\", unit=\"file\")\n    n_ok = 0\n    n_fail = 0\n\n    sem = asyncio.Semaphore(CONCURRENCY)\n    connector = aiohttp.TCPConnector(force_close=True, limit=CONCURRENCY * 2)\n    async with aiohttp.ClientSession(connector=connector) as session:\n        if not await check_server(session):\n            print(f\"[ERROR] sglang server not ready at {API_BASE}\")\n            sys.exit(1)\n\n        for b0 in range(0, len(samples), BATCH_SIZE):\n            batch = samples[b0:b0 + BATCH_SIZE]\n            # Send\n            results = await asyncio.gather(\n                *[send_one(session, s, sem) for s in batch]\n            )\n            n_fail_batch = sum(1 for ext, err in results if ext is None)\n            n_ok += len(batch) - n_fail_batch\n            n_fail += n_fail_batch\n            sender_pbar.update(len(batch))\n            sender_pbar.set_postfix(ok=n_ok, fail=n_fail)\n            if n_fail_batch > 0:\n                top = sorted(_FAIL.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:3]\n                tqdm.write(f\"  batch fails: {n_fail_batch}/{len(batch)} reasons: {top}\")\n\n            # Wait for hook flush\n            await asyncio.sleep(FLUSH_WAIT_SEC)\n\n            # Process raw hook .pt files: attach assistant_mask, document_ids,\n            # extension token info; rename + move to out_dir\n            pt_files = sorted(collect_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"))\n            for local_idx, pt_path in enumerate(pt_files):\n                if local_idx >= len(batch):\n                    break\n                src_sample = batch[local_idx]\n                global_idx = b0 + local_idx\n                try:\n                    d = torch.load(pt_path, weights_only=True)\n                    # d typically has: input_ids (the 2K input + 50 ext), aux_hidden bf16\n                    # Validate shape alignment\n                    n_actual = d[\"input_ids\"].shape[-1] if hasattr(d[\"input_ids\"], \"shape\") else len(d[\"input_ids\"])\n                    n_input = len(src_sample[\"input_ids\"])\n                    # Build extended assistant_mask: original mask + all-True for the 50-tok ext\n                    # (we want LM head row 0 / 73440 to learn from post-EOS labels)\n                    ext_mask = src_sample[\"assistant_mask\"] + [1] * (n_actual - n_input)\n                    ext_mask = ext_mask[:n_actual]\n                    ext_doc_ids = src_sample[\"document_ids\"] + [src_sample.get(\"n_docs\", 1)] * (n_actual - n_input)\n                    ext_doc_ids = ext_doc_ids[:n_actual]\n                    d[\"assistant_mask\"] = torch.tensor(ext_mask, dtype=torch.bool)\n                    d[\"document_ids\"] = torch.tensor(ext_doc_ids, dtype=torch.int32)\n                    d[\"source\"] = src_sample.get(\"source\", \"?\")\n                    d[\"bucket\"] = src_sample.get(\"bucket\", n_input)\n                    d[\"n_input\"] = n_input\n                    d[\"n_total\"] = n_actual\n\n                    src = src_sample.get(\"source\", \"unk\").replace(\"/\", \"_\")\n                    bucket = src_sample.get(\"bucket\", n_input)\n                    out_name = f\"{global_idx:07d}_{src}_b{bucket}.pt\"\n                    out_path = out_dir / out_name\n                    tmp = out_path.with_suffix(out_path.suffix + \".tmp\")\n                    torch.save(d, tmp)\n                    tmp.replace(out_path)\n                    pt_path.unlink()\n                    save_pbar.update(1)\n                except Exception as e:\n                    tqdm.write(f\"  process err {pt_path.name}: {e}\")\n                    pt_path.unlink()\n\n    sender_pbar.close()\n    save_pbar.close()\n\n    print(f\"\\n[collect_v4] done. ok={n_ok}, fail={n_fail}\")\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] output: {out_dir} ({len(list(out_dir.glob('*.pt')))} files)\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    asyncio.run(main())\n"
}

383	    def _build_target_p(self, target_logits_values, target_logits_indices, device):
384	        """Build target distribution from top-256 logits.
385	
386	        Maps top-256 full-vocab logits into draft vocab space, applies softmax.
387	        Mirrors official: target_head = full_logits[..., t2d]; target_p = softmax(target_head)
388	        We approximate by scattering top-256 logits into draft vocab positions.
389	        """
390	        B, S, K = target_logits_values.shape
391	        indices = target_logits_indices.long().clamp(0, VOCAB_SIZE - 1)
392	        in_draft = self.t2d[indices]  # (B, S, 256) bool
393	
394	        # Build draft-space logits: only scatter tokens that are in draft vocab
395	        # Use reduce='amax' to avoid race condition when multiple entries map to same idx
396	        draft_logits = torch.full((B, S, DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE), -1e9, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
397	        mapped_idx = self.draft_idx_map[indices]  # (B, S, 256)
398	        values = target_logits_values.float()
399	
400	        # Set non-draft entries to -1e9, then scatter with amax (max wins, -1e9 never beats valid)
401	        safe_vals = torch.where(in_draft, values, torch.tensor(-1e9, device=device))
402	        draft_logits.scatter_reduce_(2, mapped_idx, safe_vals, reduce="amax")
403	        target_p = F.softmax(draft_logits, dim=-1)
404	        return target_p
405	
406	    def forward(self, token_ids, aux_hidden, target_logits_values, target_logits_indices):
407	        """
408	        EAGLE-3 training with shifted alignment to match inference.
409	
410	        Inference pattern: (embed(x_{t+1}), fc(aux[t])) → predict x_{t+2}
411	        So we shift inputs: position t gets token[t+1] with hidden[t], target[t+1].
412	
413	        1. Shift: input_ids = token_ids[:,1:], aux = aux_hidden[:,:-1], target = target[:,1:]
414	        2. fc(aux) → hidden
415	        3. For each TTT step:
416	           a. embed(input_ids) → input_emb
417	           b. midlayer(input_emb, hidden, cache) → hidden_out
418	           c. norm(hidden_out) → lm_head → logits
419	           d. plogp loss with position_mask
420	           e. Shift input_ids, target, loss by 1 position (padding left=False)
421	        """
422	        B, S_orig = token_ids.shape
423	        device = token_ids.device
424	
425	        # Shift alignment: match inference pattern (x_{t+1}, aux[t]) → predict x_{t+2}
426	        input_ids = token_ids[:, 1:]           # (B, S-1): tokens x_1..x_{S-1}
427	        aux_shifted = aux_hidden[:, :-1, :]    # (B, S-1, 12288): aux_0..aux_{S-2}
428	        target_values = target_logits_values[:, 1:, :]   # (B, S-1, K)
429	        target_indices = target_logits_indices[:, 1:, :]  # (B, S-1, K)
430	        S = S_orig - 1
431	
432	        hidden = self.fc(aux_shifted)
433	
434	        # Build causal mask once (same sequence length throughout)
435	        causal_mask = self._make_causal_mask(S, device)
436	
437	        # Base position IDs — offset by step * S each TTT step (matching original position_ids + lck)
438	        base_position_ids = torch.arange(S, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(B, -1)
439	
440	        step_losses = []
441	        step_accs = []
442	        cache_k_list = None
443	        cache_v_list = None
444	
445	        for step in range(self.ttt_steps):
446	            last = step == self.ttt_steps - 1
447	
448	            # Position IDs with cache length offset (original: position_ids + lck)
449	            lck = step * S  # cache length from prior TTT steps
450	            position_ids = base_position_ids + lck
451	
452	            # Embed current input_ids
453	            input_emb = self.embed_tokens(input_ids) * self.scale_emb
454	            input_emb = input_emb.to(hidden.dtype)
455	
456	            # Forward through decoder layer with list-based KV cache
457	            if GRAD_CHECKPOINT and self.training:
458	                def _fwd(ie, hs, cm, pos, *cache_tensors):
459	                    # Reconstruct lists from flattened tensors
460	                    n = len(cache_tensors) // 2
461	                    ck = list(cache_tensors[:n]) if n > 0 else None
462	                    cv = list(cache_tensors[n:]) if n > 0 else None
463	                    out, new_ck, new_cv = self.midlayer(ie, hs, ck, cv, cm, pos)
464	                    return (out, *new_ck, *new_cv)
465	
466	                # Flatten cache lists for checkpoint (it needs tensors, not lists)
467	                cache_tensors = []
468	                if cache_k_list is not None:
469	                    cache_tensors = [*cache_k_list, *cache_v_list]
470	                results = torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(
471	                    _fwd, input_emb, hidden, causal_mask, position_ids, *cache_tensors,
472	                    use_reentrant=False,
473	                )
474	                hidden_out = results[0]
475	                n_cache = (len(results) - 1) // 2
476	                cache_k_list = list(results[1:1+n_cache])
477	                cache_v_list = list(results[1+n_cache:])
478	            else:
479	                hidden_out, cache_k_list, cache_v_list = self.midlayer(
480	                    input_emb, hidden, cache_k_list, cache_v_list, causal_mask, position_ids,
481	                )
482	            hidden = hidden_out
483	
484	            # Compute target distribution for this step's target
485	            with torch.no_grad():
486	                target_p = self._build_target_p(target_values, target_indices, device)
487	                # target_p: (B, S, draft_vocab)
488	
489	                # Position mask: only positions where target argmax is in draft vocab
490	                target_argmax_full = target_indices[:, :, 0].long().clamp(0, VOCAB_SIZE - 1)  # top-1
491	                target_mask = self.t2d[target_argmax_full].float()  # (B, S)
492	
493	            # Logits
494	            normed = self.norm(hidden_out)
495	            logits = self.lm_head(normed).float()  # (B, S, draft_vocab)
496	
497	            # plogp loss (official L854-855) — mean over valid positions
498	            out_logp = F.log_softmax(logits, dim=-1)
499	            plogp = target_p * out_logp  # (B, S, draft_vocab)
500	            neg_ce = -plogp.sum(dim=-1)  # (B, S) — cross-entropy per position
501	            # Average only over positions where target is in draft vocab
502	            n_valid = target_mask.sum().clamp(min=1)
503	            loss = (neg_ce * target_mask).sum() / n_valid
504	            step_losses.append(loss)
505	
506	            # Accuracy
507	            with torch.no_grad():
508	                pred_idx = logits.argmax(-1)  # (B, S)
509	                target_draft_idx = target_p.argmax(-1)  # (B, S)
510	                correct = (pred_idx == target_draft_idx).float() * target_mask
511	                acc = correct.sum().item() / (target_mask.sum().item() + 1e-6)
512	                step_accs.append(acc)
513	
514	            # Shift for next step (official L862-864)
515	            if not last:
516	                # padding(x, left=False) = cat(x[:,1:], zeros)
517	                input_ids = torch.cat([input_ids[:, 1:], torch.zeros(B, 1, dtype=input_ids.dtype, device=device)], dim=1)
518	                target_values = torch.cat([target_values[:, 1:, :], torch.zeros(B, 1, target_values.shape[2], dtype=target_values.dtype, device=device)], dim=1)
519	                target_indices = torch.cat([target_indices[:, 1:, :], torch.zeros(B, 1, target_indices.shape[2], dtype=target_indices.dtype, device=device)], dim=1)
520	
521	        # Weighted total loss
522	        total_loss = sum(self.loss_decay ** i * l for i, l in enumerate(step_losses))
523	        return total_loss, step_losses, step_accs
524	
525	
526	# ── Data loading ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
527	# nvfp4_codec is imported lazily (worker threads) to avoid module-level torch ops
528	# on main process at import time.
529	_NVFP4_CODEC = None
530	
531	
532	_NVFP4_PAYLOAD_KEYS = {"token_ids", "aux_packed", "aux_scale",
```

────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef aoi_position_ids(seq_len: int, batch_size: int, device, training: bool = True) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"LongSpec Anchor-Offset Indices.\n\n    sink ~ U[0, AOI_MAX_SINK]\n    offset ~ U[0, AOI_CAP - seq_len]\n    pos = arange(seq_len), pos[sink:] += offset\n    \"\"\"\n    if not training or seq_len >= AOI_CAP:\n        return torch.arange(seq_len, device=device).unsqueeze(0).expand(batch_size, -1)\n\n    sink = random.randint(0, AOI_MAX_SINK)\n    offset_max = max(0, AOI_CAP - seq_len)\n    offset = random.randint(0, offset_max)\n    pos = torch.arange(seq_len, device=device)\n    pos[sink:] += offset\n    return pos.unsqueeze(0).expand(batch_size, -1)\n\n\n# ── Cross-doc attention mask ───────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef build_attention_mask_with_docs(seq_len: int, document_ids: torch.Tensor,\n                                    device) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"Combine causal mask with cross-doc mask.\n\n    document_ids: (B, S) per-token doc id\n    Returns: (B, 1, S, S) additive mask (-inf where blocked, 0 otherwise)\n    \"\"\"\n    B, S = document_ids.shape\n    # Causal: lower triangular\n    causal = torch.tril(torch.ones(S, S, device=device))  # (S, S)\n    # Cross-doc: keep only when doc_id_q == doc_id_k\n    doc_eq = (document_ids.unsqueeze(2) == document_ids.unsqueeze(1)).float()  # (B, S, S)\n    combined = causal.unsqueeze(0) * doc_eq  # (B, S, S)\n    additive = torch.where(combined > 0, 0.0, float(\"-inf\"))\n    return additive.unsqueeze(1)  # (B, 1, S, S)\n\n\n# ── LK^λ loss ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef lk_lambda_loss(target_p: torch.Tensor, draft_logits: torch.Tensor,\n                    eta: float = LK_ETA, mask: torch.Tensor | None = None) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"LK^λ loss (arXiv:2602.23881).\n\n    Args:\n        target_p: (B, S, V) target probability distribution\n        draft_logits: (B, S, V) draft model logits (will softmax internally)\n        mask: (B, S) bool — only positions where mask=True contribute\n        eta: schedule parameter\n\n    Returns scalar loss.\n    \"\"\"\n    log_q = F.log_softmax(draft_logits, dim=-1)\n    q = log_q.exp()                                       # draft probs\n    # KL(p || q) = sum p * (log p - log q)\n    log_p = (target_p + 1e-12).log()\n    kl = (target_p * (log_p - log_q)).sum(dim=-1)         # (B, S)\n    # TV(p, q) = 0.5 * sum |p - q|\n    tv = 0.5 * (target_p - alpha)                          # (B, S)\n    per_pos = lam * kl + (1 - lam) * tv                    # (B, S)\n    if mask is not None:\n        m = mask.float()\n        n_valid = m.sum().clamp(min=1.0)\n        return (per_pos * m).sum() / n_valid\n    return per_pos.mean()\n\n\n# ── V4 Model: extends v3 model with cross-doc + assistant mask aware forward ──\n\nclass \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, embed_weight, lm_head_weight):\n        super().__init__(embed_weight, lm_head_weight)\n        # Override RoPE cache to support large positions (226K)\n        # build_attention_mask_with_docs(S, document_ids, device)\n\n        total_loss = sum(self.loss_decay ** i * l for i, l in enumerate(step_losses))\n        return total_loss, step_losses, step_accs\n\n\n# ── V4 dataloader (bf16 .pt) ───────────────────────────────────────────\n\ndef load_sample_v4(pt_path: Path, device: str = DEVICE) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Load v4 .pt file: bf16 aux, with    # Fallback to v3 vocab if not built yet (smoke test only)\n        print(f\"[v4] WARNING: {vocab_cache} not found, falling back to v3 build_vocab_mapping\")\n        model.build_vocab_mapping(data_dir)\n    v3train.init_mlp_from_target(model)\n\n    # Load dataset\n    files = sorted(data_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"))\n    print(f\"[v4] found {len(files)} .pt files in []\n\n    file_iter = iter(files)\n    cur_step = 0\n    for step_i in pbar:\n        opt.zero_grad()\n        accum_loss = 0.0\n        accum_step0 = 0.0\n        accum_n = 0\n        for _ in range(GRAD_ACCUM):\n            try:\n                batch_files = []\n                for _ in range(BATCH_SIZE):\n                    f = next(file_iter)\n                    batch_files.append(f)\n            except StopIteration:\n                file_iter = iter(files)\n                random.shuffle(files)\n                continue\n\n            # Load batch\n            try:\n                samples = [load_sample_v4(f) for f in batch_files]\n            except Exception as e:\n                tqdm.write(f\"  load err: {e}\")\n                continue\n\n            # Pad to common length\n            max_len = max(s[\"input_ids\"].shape[-1] for s in samples)\n            max_len = min(max_len, SEQ_LEN_MAX)\n\n            def to_tensor(samples, key, pad_value):\n                B = len(samples)\n                ts = []\n                for s in samples:\n                    t = s[key]\n                    if t.shape[-1] > max_len:\n                        t = t[..., :max_len]\n                    elif t.shape[-1] < max_len:\n                        pad_shape = list(t.shape)\n                        pad_shape[-1] = max_len - t.shape[-1]\n                        pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype, device=DEVICE)\n                        t = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=-1)\n                    ts.append(t)\n                return torch.stack(ts).to(DEVICE)\n\n            tok = to_tensor(samples, \"input_ids\", 0).long()\n            aux = to_tensor(samples, \"aux_hidden\", 0.0).to(DTYPE)\n            tlv = to_tensor(samples, \"target_logit_values\", 0.0).to(torch.float32)\n            tli = to_tensor(samples, \"target_logit_indices\", 0).long()\n            am = to_tensor(samples, \"assistant_mask\", 0).bool() if \"assistant_mask\" in samples[0] else None\n            di = to_tensor(samples, \"document_ids\", 0).long() if \"document_ids\" in samples[0] else None\n\n            try:\n                total_loss, step_losses, step_accs = model(\n                    tok, aux, tlv, tli, assistant_mask=am, document_ids=di\n                )\n                loss = total_loss / GRAD_ACCUM\n                loss.backward()\n                accum_loss += total_loss.item()\n                accum_step0 += step_accs[0]\n                accum_n += 1\n            except Exception as e:\n                tqdm.write(f\"  forward err: {e}\")\n                opt.zero_grad()\n                continue\n\n        if accum_n == 0:\n            continue\n        gn = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), MAX_GRAD_NORM)\n        opt.step()\n        sched.step()\n\n        losses.append(accum_loss / accum_n)\n        grad_norms.append(gn.item())\n        step_0_accs.append(accum_step0 / accum_n)\n        pbar.set_postfix(\n            loss=f\"{losses[-1]:.3f}\",\n            gn=f\"{grad_norms[-1]:.2f}\",\n            acc0=f\"{step_0_accs[-1]:.3f}\",\n        )\n\n        if math.isnan(losses[-1]) or math.isinf(losses[-1]):\n            tqdm.write(f\"  loss NaN/Inf at step {step_i}, abort\")\n            break\n\n    pbar.close()\n\n    # Save smoke trace\n    log_path = output_dir / \"smoke_trace.json\"\n    import json\n    with open(log_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump({\n            \"loss\": losses,\n            \"grad_norm\": grad_norms,\n            \"step_0_acc\": step_0_accs,\n        }, f, indent=1)\n    print(f\"[v4] smoke done. saved trace → {log_path}\")\n    print(f\"[v4] loss: first={losses[0]:.3f}, last={losses[-1]:.3f}, min={min(losses):.3f}\")\n    print(f\"[v4] step_0_acc: first={step_0_accs[0]:.3f}, last={step_0_accs[-1]:.3f}, max={max(step_0_accs):.3f}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/validation/v4_data_check.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"V4 data sanity histogram printer (no hard thresholds — distribution judgement).\n\nReads .pt files from eagle/data/train_v4/, prints distribution of:\n    - per-sample 73440 / 73441 / 0 (unk) counts\n    - assistant_mask coverage ratio\n    - bucket distribution\n    - aux_hidden shape & dtype consistency\n    - document count per sample (for multi-doc pack)\n    - presence of post-EOS extension (last 50 tokens characteristic)\n\nRun:\n    python3 eagle/validation/v4_data_check.py [--data_dir eagle/data/train_v4]\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nfrom collections import Counter, defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport torch\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--data_dir\", default=\"eagle/data/train_v4\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--max_files\", type=int, default=200, help=\"0 = all\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    data_dir = Path(args.data_dir)\n    files = sorted(data_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"))\n    if args.max_files > 0:\n        files = files[:args.max_files]\n    print(f\"[check] reading {len(files)} files from {data_dir}\")\n    if not files:\n        print(\"[check] no files. exiting.\")\n        return\n\n    # Counters\n    bucket_counts = Counter()\n    src_counts = Counter()\n    has_imend = 0\n    has_imstart = 0\n    has_unk = 0\n    n_imend_per = []\n    n_imstart_per = []\n    n_unk_per = []\n    n_total_tokens = 0\n    asst_coverage = []\n    n_docs_per = []\n    aux_dtypes = Counter()\n    aux_shapes = Counter()\n    last_50_unk_or_imend = 0\n\n    for fp in files:\n        try:\n            d = torch.load(fp, weights_only=True)\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"  load err {fp.name}: {e}\")\n            continue\n        toks = d[\"input_ids\"] if torch.is_tensor(d[\"input_ids\"]) else torch.tensor(d[\"input_ids\"])\n        toks = toks.flatten().cpu()\n        n = toks.numel()\n        n_total_tokens += n\n        bucket = d.get(\"bucket\", n)\n        bucket_counts[bucket] += 1\n        src_counts[d.get(\"source\", \"?\")] += 1\n\n        c0 = (toks == 0).sum().item()\n        c40 = (toks == 73440).sum().item()\n        c41 = (toks == 73441).sum().item()\n        n_unk_per.append(c0)\n        n_imend_per.append(c40)\n        n_imstart_per.append(c41)\n        if c0 > 0: has_unk += 1\n        if c40 > 0: has_imend += 1\n        if c41 > 0: has_imstart += 1\n\n        if \"assistant_mask\" in d:\n            am = d[\"assistant_mask\"]\n            if torch.is_tensor(am):\n                am = am.flatten().cpu().bool()\n            else:\n                am = torch.tensor(am).bool()\n            asst_coverage.append(am.float().mean().item())\n\n        if \"document_ids\" in d:\n            doc_ids = d[\"document_ids\"]\n            if torch.is_tensor(doc_ids):\n                doc_ids = doc_ids.flatten().cpu()\n            n_docs_per.append(int(doc_ids.max()) + 1)\n\n        if \"aux_hidden\" in d:\n            aux = d[\"aux_hidden\"]\n            aux_dtypes[str(aux.dtype)] += 1\n            aux_shapes[tuple(aux.shape)] += 1\n\n        # post-EOS check: last 50 tokens\n        last50 = toks[-50:].tolist()\n        if 0 in last50 or sum(1 for t in last50 if t == 73440) >= 3:\n            last_50_unk_or_imend += 1\n\n    n = len(files)\n    print(\"\\n========================================\")\n    print(f\"Total samples: {n}, total tokens: {n_total_tokens}\")\n    print(\"\\n=== Bucket distribution ===\")\n    for k, v in sorted(bucket_counts.items()):\n        print(f\"  {k:>6}  {v}\")\n    print(\"\\n=== Source distribution ===\")\n    for k, v in sorted(src_counts.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):\n        print(f\"  {k:25} {v}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n=== Special token presence ===\")\n    print(f\"  Samples with >=1 <|im_end|> (73440):  {has_imend}/{n} ({has_imend/n*100:.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Samples with >=1 <|im_start|> (73441):{has_imstart}/{n} ({has_imstart/n*100:.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  Samples with >=1 <unk> (0):           {has_unk}/{n} ({has_unk/n*100:.1f}%)\")\n    print(f\"  total <|im_end|>: {sum(n_imend_per)}, mean per sample: {sum(n_imend_per)/n:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  total <|im_start|>: {sum(n_imstart_per)}, mean per sample: {sum(n_imstart_per)/n:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  total <unk>: {sum(n_unk_per)}, mean per sample: {sum(n_unk_per)/n:.2f}\")\n    print(f\"  Samples with last-50 containing post-EOS pattern (unk OR >=3 imend): {last_50_unk_or_imend}/{n} ({last_50_unk_or_imend/n*100:.1f}%)\")\n\n    if asst_coverage:\n        ac = sorted(asst_coverage)\n        m = len(ac) // 2\n        print(f\"\\n=== Assistant-mask coverage ===\")\n        print(f\"  mean: {sum(ac)/len(ac):.3f}\")\n        print(f\"  p10/p50/p90: {ac[len(ac)//10]:.3f} / {ac[m]:.3f} / {ac[-len(ac)//10-1]:.3f}\")\n\n    if n_docs_per:\n        nd = sorted(n_docs_per)\n        m = len(nd) // 2\n        print(f\"\\n=== Documents per sample (multi-doc pack) ===\")\n        print(f\"  mean: {sum(nd)/len(nd):.2f}, max: {max(nd)}, p50: {nd[m]}\")\n        ndc = Counter(nd)\n        print(\"  count distribution:\")\n        for k in sorted(ndc.keys())[:15]:\n            print(f\"    {k} docs: {ndc[k]} samples\")\n\n    if aux_dtypes:\n        print(\"\\n=== aux_hidden dtypes ===\")\n        for k, v in aux_dtypes.most_common():\n            print(f\"  {k}: {v}\")\n        print(\"=== aux_hidden shape diversity ===\")\n        for k, v in list(aux_shapes.most_common())[:10]:\n            print(f\"  {k}: {v}\")\n\n    print(\"\\n[check] done.\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/pipeline/build_prompts_v4.py",
  "old_string": "def load_chinese_r1() -> list[tuple[list[dict], str]]:\n    path = \"/user_4813494d/data/eagle_raw/chinese_r1_distill/distill_r1_110k.jsonl\"\n    out = []\n    with open(path) as f:\n        for line in tqdm(f, desc=\"  read chinese_r1\", total=110000):\n            r = json.loads(line)\n            if (r.get(\"score\") or 0) < 7:\n                continue\n            user = r.get(\"input\") or \"\"\n            think = r.get(\"reasoning_content\") or \"\"\n            ans = r.get(\"content\") or \"\"\n            if not user or (not think and not ans):\n                continue\n            asst = \"\"\n            if think:\n                asst += f\"<think>\\n{think}\\n</think>\\n\\n\"\n            if ans:\n                asst += ans\n            msgs = [\n                {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user},\n                {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": asst},\n            ]\n            grp = r.get(\"repo_name\", \"unknown\")\n            out.append((msgs, grp))\n    return out",
  "new_string": "def load_chinese_r1(max_docs: int = 0) -> list[tuple[list[dict], str]]:\n    path = \"/user_4813494d/data/eagle_raw/chinese_r1_distill/distill_r1_110k.jsonl\"\n    out = []\n    with open(path) as f:\n        for line in tqdm(f, desc=\"  read chinese_r1\", total=110000):\n            if max_docs and len(out) >= max_docs:\n                break\n            r = json.loads(line)\n            if (r.get(\"score\") or 0) < 7:\n                continue\n            user = r.get(\"input\") or \"\"\n            think = r.get(\"reasoning_content\") or \"\"\n            ans = r.get(\"content\") or \"\"\n            if not user or (not think and not ans):\n                continue\n            asst = \"\"\n            if think:\n                asst += f\"<think>\\n{think}\\n</think>\\n\\n\"\n            if ans:\n                asst += ans\n            msgs = [\n                {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user},\n                {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": asst},\n            ]\n            grp = r.get(\"repo_name\", \"unknown\")\n            out.append((msgs, grp))\n    return out"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/pipeline/build_prompts_v4.py",
  "old_string": "def load_stem_zh() -> list[tuple[list[dict], str]]:\n    base = Path(\"/user_4813494d/data/eagle_raw/stem_zh\")\n    out = []\n    for fname in sorted(base.glob(\"*.json\")):\n        if fname.name == \"dataset_infos.json\":\n            continue\n        subj = fname.stem.split(\"_\")[0]\n        with open(fname) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                try:\n                    r = json.loads(line)\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n                inst = r.get(\"instruction\") or \"\"\n                inp = r.get(\"input\") or \"\"\n                ans = r.get(\"output\") or \"\"\n                user = (inst + (\"\\n\" + inp if inp else \"\")).strip()\n                if not user or not ans:\n                    continue\n                msgs = [\n                    {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user},\n                    {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": ans},\n                ]\n                out.append((msgs, subj))\n    return out",
  "new_string": "def load_stem_zh(max_docs: int = 0) -> list[tuple[list[dict], str]]:\n    base = Path(\"/user_4813494d/data/eagle_raw/stem_zh\")\n    out = []\n    for fname in sorted(base.glob(\"*.json\")):\n        if fname.name == \"dataset_infos.json\":\n            continue\n        if max_docs and len(out) >= max_docs:\n            break\n        subj = fname.stem.split(\"_\")[0]\n        with open(fname) as f:\n            for line in f:\n                if max_docs and len(out) >= max_docs:\n                    break\n                try:\n                    r = json.loads(line)\n                except Exception:\n                    continue\n                inst = r.get(\"instruction\") or \"\"\n                inp = r.get(\"input\") or \"\"\n                ans = r.get(\"output\") or \"\"\n                user = (inst + (\"\\n\" + inp if inp else \"\")).strip()\n                if not user or not ans:\n                    continue\n                msgs = [\n                    {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": user},\n                    {\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": ans},\n                ]\n                out.append((msgs, subj))\n    return out"
}

#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

cd /user_4813494d/openbmb

# EAGLE-3 v3 data collection server (target-only, no speculative).
# Pair with: python3 eagle/pipeline/collect_async.py
#
# Differs from eval/start_eagle.sh:
#   - NO --speculative-* flags (pure target, hook captures aux_hidden)
#   - --chunked-prefill-size 131072 (prevents 2048-tok prompts being sliced
#     into fragments; see docs/eagle/training-v2.md trap #1)
#   - --max-running-requests 32 (matches collect_async.py CONCURRENCY)
#   - EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR / EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS / EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS / EAGLE3_TOP_K
#     activate the collection hook in demo-sala/.../minicpm.py
#
# Aux layers [4, 9, 24]: data-driven choice from phase-2/3 probe search
# (see eagle/probe/probe_results.json). Replaces v1/v2's [1, 10, 22].

TARGET_MODEL="${EAGLE_TARGET_MODEL:-/user_4813494d/models/openbmb/MiniCPM-SALA-gptq46-calib90-90k-NVFP4}"

EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR="${EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR:-/tmp/eagle3_collect_v2}" \
EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS="${EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS:-4,9,24}" \
EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS="${EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS:-2048}" \
EAGLE3_TOP_K="${EAGLE3_TOP_K:-128}" \
SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD="${SGLANG_MARLIN_DECODE_THRESHOLD:-48}" \
SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE="${SGLANG_MINICPM_PLAN_CACHE:-1}" \
SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE="${SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE:-/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json}" \
CUTE_DSL_ARCH="${CUTE_DSL_ARCH:-sm_120a}" \
CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR="${CUTE_DSL_CACHE_DIR:-/tmp/cute_dsl_cache}" \
SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X="${SGLANG_ENABLE_B12X:-1}" \
SGLANG_ENABLE_SPEC_V2=0 \
python3 -m sglang.launch_server \
    --model-path "${TARGET_MODEL}" \
    --trust-remote-code --port 30000 \
    --mem-fraction-static 0.75 \
    --max-running-requests 32 \
    --attention-backend minicpm_flashinfer \
    --chunked-prefill-size 131072 --disable-radix-cache \
    --skip-server-warmup \
```

358	# ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

361	# ── EAGLE-3 aux hidden + top-logit collection ─────────────────────────
362	_EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR = _os.environ.get("EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR", "")
363	_EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS = [1, 10, 22]
364	_EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS = int(_os.environ.get("EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS", "2048"))  # 0 = no limit
365	_EAGLE3_TOP_K = int(_os.environ.get("EAGLE3_TOP_K", "256"))
366	_eagle3_counter = 0
367	_eagle3_lock = _threading.Lock()
368	_eagle3_aux_cache = {}  # layer_idx -> tensor, populated during forward
369	
370	
371	def _eagle3_capture_layer(layer_idx, hidden_states):
372	    """Called inside MiniCPMModel.forward loop to capture aux layer outputs."""
373	    if not _EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR or layer_idx not in _EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS:
374	        return
375	    _eagle3_aux_cache[layer_idx] = hidden_states.detach()
376	
377	
378	def _eagle3_collect(hidden_states, input_ids, forward_batch, lm_head, scale_width):
379	    """Save aux hidden + top-K logits during EXTEND for EAGLE-3 training."""
380	    global _eagle3_counter
381	    if not _EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR or not _os.path.isdir(_EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR):
382	        return
383	    if forward_batch.forward_mode != ForwardMode.EXTEND:
384	        _eagle3_aux_cache.clear()
385	        return
386	    seq_lens = forward_batch.extend_seq_lens_cpu
387	    if seq_lens is None:
388	        _eagle3_aux_cache.clear()
389	        return
390	    if len(_eagle3_aux_cache) != len(_EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS):
391	        _eagle3_aux_cache.clear()
392	        return
393	
394	    # Compute logits for top-K extraction
395	    logits = torch.matmul(hidden_states / scale_width, lm_head.weight.T)
396	
397	    pt = 0
398	    with _eagle3_lock:
399	        for seq_len in seq_lens:
400	            if _EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS > 0:
401	                start = pt + max(0, seq_len - _EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS)
402	            else:
403	                start = pt
404	            end = pt + seq_len
405	            ids = input_ids[start:end].cpu()
406	
407	            # Concat aux layers: (tokens, 4096*3)
408	            aux_parts = []
409	            for li in _EAGLE3_AUX_LAYERS:
410	                aux_parts.append(
411	                    _eagle3_aux_cache[li][start:end].to(torch.bfloat16).cpu()
412	                )
413	            aux_hidden = torch.cat(aux_parts, dim=-1)
414	
415	            # Top-K logits
416	            seg_logits = logits[start:end].float()
417	            topk_vals, topk_ids = torch.topk(seg_logits, _EAGLE3_TOP_K, dim=-1)
418	
419	            torch.save(
420	                {
421	                    "token_ids": ids,
422	                    "aux_hidden": aux_hidden,
423	                    "top_logit_values": topk_vals.to(torch.bfloat16).cpu(),
424	                    "top_logit_indices": topk_ids.to(torch.int32).cpu(),
425	                },
426	                _os.path.join(
427	                    _EAGLE3_COLLECT_DIR, f"{_eagle3_counter:06d}.pt"
428	                ),
429	            )
430	            _eagle3_counter += 1
431	            pt += seq_len
432	    _eagle3_aux_cache.clear()

> AGENT

发现关键问题：EAGLE3 hook 只在 EXTEND（prefill）模式触发，DECODE（autoregressive）模式不捕获 → post-EOS 自延伸的 50 token 实际**不会被 hook 保存**。修方案：smoke 阶段不做 post-EOS 自延伸（`max_tokens=1`），先验证 chat template / AOI / LK^λ 三件主要改动；post-EOS 改 hook 留 v5。

简化 collect_v4：纯顺序模式，每发一条等 hook 写完再处理下一条。

> TOOL

tool_use Write
```json
{
  "file_path": "/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/pipeline/collect_v4.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"V4 collect: bf16 direct-to-local, sequential single-stream, deterministic mapping.\n\nDifferences vs collect_async.py:\n1. Reads JSONL from build_prompts_v4 (input_ids already tokenized)\n2. Sequential one-by-one send: avoids OOM on long shards + ensures stable\n   sample → .pt mapping. Long shards (32K/64K) require this to fit GPU memory.\n3. bf16 direct-write to local out_dir, NO NVFP4, NO BOS upload\n4. Save assistant_mask + document_ids alongside hook output (token_ids,\n   aux_hidden, top_logit_values, top_logit_indices)\n5. max_tokens=1, ignore_eos=False (smoke; post-EOS DECODE capture not\n   supported by current EAGLE3 hook — v5 work)\n6. Optional length filter: --max_bucket to skip long shards on tight memory\n\nRun:\n    bash eagle/start_collect_v4.sh > /tmp/v4_server.log 2>&1 &\n    # wait for \"Uvicorn running on\"\n    python3 eagle/pipeline/collect_v4.py \\\\\n        --in /tmp/eagle3_prompts_v4_smoke1k.jsonl \\\\\n        --out_dir eagle/data/train_v4 \\\\\n        --max_bucket 16384\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport requests\nimport torch\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nAPI_BASE = \"http://127.0.0.1:30000\"\n\n\ndef collect_one(sample, collect_dir, out_dir, idx):\n    \"\"\"Send one sample, wait for hook output, attach metadata, write .pt.\"\"\"\n    # Snapshot current .pt counter\n    before = set(p.name for p in collect_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"))\n\n    # Send\n    try:\n        r = requests.post(\n            f\"{API_BASE}/v1/completions\",\n            json={\n                \"model\": \"default\",\n                \"prompt\": sample[\"input_ids\"],\n                \"max_tokens\": 1,\n                \"temperature\": 0,\n                \"ignore_eos\": False,\n            },\n            timeout=600,\n        )\n        if r.status_code != 200:\n            return False, f\"http_{r.status_code}\"\n        body = r.json()\n        if \"error\" in body:\n            return False, f\"body_err:{str(body)[:80]}\"\n    except Exception as e:\n        return False, f\"req_err:{type(e).__name__}\"\n\n    # Wait briefly for hook flush\n    deadline = time.time() + 5.0\n    new_files = []\n    while time.time() < deadline:\n        cur = set(p.name for p in collect_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"))\n        new = cur - before\n        if new:\n            new_files = sorted(new)\n            break\n        time.sleep(0.05)\n    if not new_files:\n        return False, \"no_hook_output\"\n\n    # The newest one corresponds to our sample\n    src_pt = collect_dir / new_files[-1]\n    try:\n        d = torch.load(src_pt, weights_only=True)\n    except Exception as e:\n        return False, f\"load_err:{e}\"\n\n    # Validate alignment\n    n_input = len(sample[\"input_ids\"])\n    if \"token_ids\" not in d:\n        return False, f\"hook_missing_token_ids:{list(d.keys())}\"\n    n_actual = d[\"token_ids\"].shape[-1]\n    # Add metadata (mask + doc_ids may be slightly off if hook truncated,\n    # but for prefill-only with EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS≥sample_len they match)\n    n_meta = min(n_actual, n_input)\n    am = sample[\"assistant_mask\"][:n_meta] + [0] * max(0, n_actual - n_meta)\n    di = sample[\"document_ids\"][:n_meta] + [sample.get(\"n_docs\", 1)] * max(0, n_actual - n_meta)\n    d[\"assistant_mask\"] = torch.tensor(am, dtype=torch.bool)\n    d[\"document_ids\"] = torch.tensor(di, dtype=torch.int32)\n    d[\"source\"] = sample.get(\"source\", \"?\")\n    d[\"bucket\"] = sample.get(\"bucket\", n_input)\n    d[\"n_input\"] = n_input\n    d[\"n_total\"] = n_actual\n\n    src = sample.get(\"source\", \"unk\").replace(\"/\", \"_\")\n    bucket = sample.get(\"bucket\", n_input)\n    out_name = f\"{idx:07d}_{src}_b{bucket}.pt\"\n    out_path = out_dir / out_name\n    tmp = out_path.with_suffix(out_path.suffix + \".tmp\")\n    torch.save(d, tmp)\n    tmp.replace(out_path)\n    src_pt.unlink()\n\n    # Clean any leftover hook files (shouldn't exist with sequential send)\n    for f in collect_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"):\n        f.unlink()\n\n    return True, None\n\n\ndef main():\n    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    ap.add_argument(\"--in\", dest=\"in_path\", default=\"/tmp/eagle3_prompts_v4_smoke1k.jsonl\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--out_dir\", default=\"/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/data/train_v4\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--collect_dir\", default=\"/tmp/eagle3_collect_v4\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--limit\", type=int, default=0, help=\"0 = all samples\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--max_bucket\", type=int, default=0,\n                    help=\"skip samples with bucket > this (0 = no limit)\")\n    ap.add_argument(\"--start_idx\", type=int, default=0,\n                    help=\"resume from this index in input jsonl\")\n    args = ap.parse_args()\n\n    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir)\n    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    collect_dir = Path(args.collect_dir)\n    collect_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Clear stale hook files\n    for f in collect_dir.glob(\"*.pt\"):\n        f.unlink()\n\n    # Verify server\n    try:\n        r = requests.get(f\"{API_BASE}/v1/models\", timeout=10)\n        if r.status_code != 200:\n            print(f\"[ERROR] sglang server not ready: {r.status_code}\")\n            sys.exit(1)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"[ERROR] sglang server not reachable: {e}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    # Load samples\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] reading {args.in_path}\")\n    samples = []\n    with open(args.in_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            r = json.loads(line)\n            if args.max_bucket > 0 and r.get(\"bucket\", 0) > args.max_bucket:\n                continue\n            samples.append(r)\n    if args.limit > 0:\n        samples = samples[args.start_idx:args.start_idx + args.limit]\n    elif args.start_idx > 0:\n        samples = samples[args.start_idx:]\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] {len(samples)} samples to collect (max_bucket={args.max_bucket}, \"\n          f\"start_idx={args.start_idx}, limit={args.limit})\")\n\n    n_ok = 0\n    n_fail = 0\n    fail_reasons = defaultdict(int)\n    pbar = tqdm(total=len(samples), desc=\"collect\", unit=\"req\")\n    for i, s in enumerate(samples):\n        ok, reason = collect_one(s, collect_dir, out_dir, args.start_idx + i)\n        if ok:\n            n_ok += 1\n        else:\n            n_fail += 1\n            fail_reasons[reason] += 1\n            if n_fail <= 5:\n                tqdm.write(f\"  fail @ idx={args.start_idx + i} bucket={s.get('bucket')}: {reason}\")\n        pbar.update(1)\n        pbar.set_postfix(ok=n_ok, fail=n_fail)\n    pbar.close()\n\n    print(f\"\\n[collect_v4] done. ok={n_ok}, fail={n_fail}\")\n    if fail_reasons:\n        print(f\"[collect_v4] fail reasons:\")\n        for k, v in sorted(fail_reasons.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):\n            print(f\"  {k}: {v}\")\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] output: {out_dir} ({len(list(out_dir.glob('*.pt')))} files)\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

  "new_string": "    # Sort samples by bucket ascending so short ones process first (less risk of OOM\n    # blocking subsequent ones; failures on long shards don't waste short progress)\n    samples = sorted(samples, key=lambda s: s.get(\"bucket\", 0))\n    if args.limit > 0:\n        samples = samples[args.start_idx:args.start_idx + args.limit]\n    elif args.start_idx > 0:\n        samples = samples[args.start_idx:]\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] {len(samples)} samples to collect (max_bucket={args.max_bucket}, \"\n          f\"start_idx={args.start_idx}, limit={args.limit})\")\n    bucket_dist = defaultdict(int)\n    for s in samples:\n        bucket_dist[s.get(\"bucket\", 0)] += 1\n    print(f\"[collect_v4] bucket distribution after sort:\")\n    for k in sorted(bucket_dist.keys()):\n        print(f\"    {k}: {bucket_dist[k]}\")"

616	def load_embed_and_lm_head():
617	    """Load frozen embed_tokens and lm_head from target model."""
618	    import json
619	    config_path = os.path.join(MODEL_PATH, "config.json")
620	    with open(config_path) as f:
621	        config = json.load(f)
622	
623	    # Load from safetensors
624	    index_path = os.path.join(MODEL_PATH, "model.safetensors.index.json")
625	    if os.path.exists(index_path):
626	        with open(index_path) as f:
627	            index = json.load(f)
628	        weight_map = index["weight_map"]
629	
630	        emb_file = weight_map.get("model.embed_tokens.weight", None)
631	        lm_file = weight_map.get("lm_head.weight", None)
632	
633	        with safe_open(os.path.join(MODEL_PATH, emb_file), framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
634	            embed_weight = f.get_tensor("model.embed_tokens.weight").float()
635	
636	        if lm_file:
637	            with safe_open(os.path.join(MODEL_PATH, lm_file), framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
638	                lm_weight = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight").float()
639	        else:
640	            # tied embeddings
641	            lm_weight = embed_weight.clone()
642	    else:
643	        # Single safetensors file
644	        st_path = os.path.join(MODEL_PATH, "model.safetensors")
645	        with safe_open(st_path, framework="pt", device="cpu") as f:
646	            embed_weight = f.get_tensor("model.embed_tokens.weight").float()
647	            try:
648	                lm_weight = f.get_tensor("lm_head.weight").float()
649	            except:
650	                lm_weight = embed_weight.clone()
651	
652	    print(f"Loaded embed_tokens: {embed_weight.shape}, lm_head: {lm_weight.shape}")
653	    return embed_weight, lm_weight
654	
655	
656	def init_mlp_from_target(model: "Eagle3Model") -> None:
657	    """Initialize MLP and o_proj weights from BF16 target model layer.
658	
659	    Layers that share the same dimensions as target model layer 16:
660	      midlayer.mlp.gate_proj   (16384, 4096)
661	      midlayer.mlp.up_proj     (16384, 4096)
662	      midlayer.mlp.down_proj   (4096, 16384)
663	      midlayer.self_attn.o_proj (4096, 4096)
664	
665	    fc / q/k/v_proj / lm_head have different dims and stay randomly initialized.
666	    Starting from target BF16 weights gives the FP4-QAT a warm start, reducing
667	    epochs needed and improving final accept rate.
668	    """
669	    if not os.path.isdir(BF16_MODEL_PATH):
670	        print(f"  BF16 model not found at {BF16_MODEL_PATH}, skipping MLP init")
```

  "old_string": "            def to_tensor(samples, key, pad_value):\n                B = len(samples)\n                ts = []\n                for s in samples:\n                    t = s[key]\n                    if t.shape[-1] > max_len:\n                        t = t[..., :max_len]\n                    elif t.shape[-1] < max_len:\n                        pad_shape = list(t.shape)\n                        pad_shape[-1] = max_len - t.shape[-1]\n                        pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype, device=DEVICE)\n                        t = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=-1)\n                    ts.append(t)\n                return torch.stack(ts).to(DEVICE)",
  "new_string": "            def to_tensor(samples, key, pad_value):\n                \"\"\"Pad along sequence dimension (always dim 0 of per-sample tensor).\"\"\"\n                ts = []\n                for s in samples:\n                    t = s[key]\n                    seq_len = t.shape[0]\n                    if seq_len > max_len:\n                        t = t[:max_len]\n                    elif seq_len < max_len:\n                        pad_shape = list(t.shape)\n                        pad_shape[0] = max_len - seq_len\n                        pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype, device=t.device)\n                        t = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=0)\n                    ts.append(t)\n                return torch.stack(ts).to(DEVICE)"

  "old_string": "    def load_v4_vocab_cache(self, cache_path: Path):\n        \"\"\"Override v3's build_vocab_mapping with force-included d2t.\"\"\"\n        d = torch.load(cache_path, weights_only=True)\n        d2t = d[\"d2t\"]\n        selected_ids = d[\"selected_ids\"]\n        # t2d: target_id → True if in draft subset\n        t2d = torch.zeros(VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.bool)\n        t2d[selected_ids] = True\n        # draft_idx_map: target_id → its slot in [0, 32000) or 0 if not in subset\n        draft_idx_map = torch.zeros(VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long)\n        for slot, tid in enumerate(selected_ids.tolist()):\n            draft_idx_map[tid] = slot\n        self.register_buffer(\"t2d\", t2d)\n        self.register_buffer(\"draft_idx_map\", draft_idx_map)\n        self.register_buffer(\"d2t\", d2t)\n        print(f\"[v4 vocab] loaded {cache_path}: {len(selected_ids)} draft tokens, \"\n              f\"forced={d['forced_ids']}, coverage={d['coverage_pct']:.4%}\")",
  "new_string": "    def load_v4_vocab_cache(self, cache_path: Path):\n        \"\"\"Override v3's build_vocab_mapping with force-included d2t.\"\"\"\n        d = torch.load(cache_path, weights_only=True)\n        d2t = d[\"d2t\"]\n        selected_ids = d[\"selected_ids\"]\n        # t2d: target_id → True if in draft subset\n        t2d = torch.zeros(VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.bool)\n        t2d[selected_ids] = True\n        # draft_idx_map: target_id → its slot in [0, 32000) or 0 if not in subset\n        draft_idx_map = torch.zeros(VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long)\n        for slot, tid in enumerate(selected_ids.tolist()):\n            draft_idx_map[tid] = slot\n        # Move to whichever device the model already lives on (model.to(DEVICE) ran before this)\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\n        self.register_buffer(\"t2d\", t2d.to(device))\n        self.register_buffer(\"draft_idx_map\", draft_idx_map.to(device))\n        self.register_buffer(\"d2t\", d2t.to(device))\n        print(f\"[v4 vocab] loaded {cache_path}: {len(selected_ids)} draft tokens, \"\n              f\"forced={d['forced_ids']}, coverage={d['coverage_pct']:.4%}, device={device}\")"
}

> AGENT

## V4 重训练 session 全工作汇总

### 1. 综述与决策
- 4 个 sonnet agent 并行综述：LK loss / 长 ctx 位置编码 / draft 训练方法 / 长 ctx 数据 — 报告存 `/user_4813494d/openbmb/research/longspec/survey_*.md`
- 第二轮 sonnet agent 长 ctx 数据深挖（ProLong / LongAlpaca / LongAlign / Yi / Qwen / DeepSeek / OWL / SpecExtend / Chinese 数据集 + 跨文档 mask 决策）
- 自己读 LongSpec 论文 + 仓库代码（AOI 实现细节、Flash Noisy Training 算法、二阶段训练 recipe）
- 5 源训练数据探针（500/源）：tokenizer 特殊 token / chat template / unk-imend 频率 / role 边界

### 2. 关键发现（推翻旧假设）
- **vocab 73440 不是 73448**：73441-73447 是 added tokens，draft d2t 子集结构性缺 `<|im_end|>`/`<|im_start|>`
- v2/v3 build_prompts.py **从未应用 chat template**——训练 label 0 个 `<|im_end|>`，与生产推理 mismatch
- `<unk>` 在训练数据**自然 0 出现**——必须人为引入（target self-extension）
- bench 实测最长 = 136,770 tokens → **AOI cap 144K** 即够（256K 浪费 RoPE cache）
- ProLong ablation 证：multi-doc pack **必须 cross-doc attention mask**（与 LongSpec 默认相反）

### 3. plan 落地（plan 文件 → 5 个 commit on `demosala-rollback`）

| Milestone | Commit | 内容 |
|---|---|---|
| **M0** | `485a8d9` | 带宽实测 12.3 MB/s avg, ModelScope SDK `max_workers=8` 拉 AM-DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-1.4M `.zst` 5.0 GB / 2 min |
| **M1** | `f6ac757` | `build_prompts_v4.py`（5 源 chat template + multi-doc pack + cross-doc id 跟踪 + AM-DeepSeek zh-long stream + decontaminate） + `vocab_cache_v4.py`（force-include `{0, 73440, 73441}`） + `collect_v4.py` + `train_v4.py` 脚手架 |
| **M3** | `f92eb99` | `start_collect_v4.sh` + `collect_v4.py` 序列化采集（concurrency=1 + sort by bucket asc）+ 600 short shard 数据集 + sanity OK |
| **M5** | `bf093a8` | `train_v4.py` bug fix（pad seq dim 0 / vocab_cache device move / token_ids key fallback） + 50 步 smoke train PASS：loss 21.528 → 10.923, step_0_acc 0 → 0.164 |
| **M8** | `a10c2a6` | `docs/eagle/training-v4.md` 完整化（决策表、实现细节、实测数据、known issues、next-iter TODO） |

### 4. 验证通过的核心改动
- **AOI 位置编码** cap=144K, sink ∈ randint(0,4)：unit test + 50 步 train OK
- **LK^λ loss** η=5：`λ·KL + (1−λ)·TV`, `λ=exp(-5·sg[α])` — gradient finite, 收敛
- **Response-only mask**：assistant_mask 覆盖 mean=0.89
- **Cross-doc attention mask**：document_ids 跟踪 0-18 docs/sample，attn mask 阻断 cross-doc 验证 OK
- **Chat template**：80.5% sample 含 73440，81% 含 73441
- **Vocab force-include**：3 个特殊 id 进 32K 子集，coverage 99.86%

### 5. 已知阻塞（v4 next-iter）
- **Server OOM on 8K+ shard**：`modelopt_fp4 + InfLLM-v2 sparse` 在长 shard `compressed_attention` 1012 MiB 分配失败。`max_running_requests=2` 仍崩。建议改 BF16 target 或单 sample 串行 + GPU mem 清理
- **Post-EOS DECODE 不捕获**：现 EAGLE3 hook 仅 EXTEND（prefill）触发；要让 `<unk>` 进 label 需改 `demo-sala/.../minicpm.py:_eagle3_collect`
- 5K full + 200 步 train + 长 ctx 中文 shard 全部依赖上面两条

### 6. 产出文件清单
- 代码：`eagle/pipeline/{build_prompts_v4,vocab_cache_v4,collect_v4}.py`、`eagle/{train_v4,start_collect_v4.sh}`、`eagle/validation/v4_data_check.py`
- 数据：`eagle/data/vocab_cache_v4.pt` (502 KB)、`eagle/data/train_v4/` (600 .pt, 29 GB)、`eagle/weights/v4/smoke_trace.json`
- 外源：`research/longspec/datasets/.../AM-DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-1.4M/{am_0.5M,am_0.9M}.jsonl.zst` (5.0 GB)
- 文档：`docs/eagle/training-v4.md`、`research/longspec/survey_*.md` (5 篇)
- Plan：`/user_4813494d/.claude/plans/1-chunk-2-glowing-quilt.md`

## 估计依据

### 数据来源
跑过的 probe（在 [Earlier turn](#)）：用 AM-team 提供的 stratified `am_0.9M_sample_1k.jsonl`（1000 样本）流式分析：

```
Zh: 92/1000 = 9.2%

长度分布（char-level token 估）:
  <2k:    156 (15.6%)
  2k-8k:  743 (74.3%)
  8k-16k:  73 (7.3%)
  16k-32k: 27 (2.7%)
  32k+:     1 (0.1%)

Zh × ≥8K: 5 个
  ~17215 tok  codeio
  ~13788 tok  evol-zh
  ~12489 tok  InfinityInstruct
  ~9136  tok  evol-zh
  ~8937  tok  InfinityInstruct
```

### 实际外推
- 1.4M × 9.2% = **128,800 中文样本** ✓（130K 估对了）
- 1.4M × 5/1000 = **7,000 中文 × ≥8K** （我之前 13K 估**错**）
- 1.4M × 1/1000 = **1,400 中文 × ≥16K**（更悲观）

### 校准修正
| 阈值 | 1K 命中 | 全量外推 | 之前我说的 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Zh × ≥8K | 5 / 1000 | **~7K** | 13K（高估 1.85×）|
| Zh × ≥16K | 1 / 1000 | **~1.4K** | — |
| Zh × ≥32K | 0 / 1000 | 估 ~0-数百 | — |

我之前那个 13K 来源：agent #4 的"9% × 10% long" — 那个 10% 是**全语言**长样本占比，错误地把它套到 Chinese subset 上。真实 Chinese subset 可能比英文 subset 短（Chinese reasoning 平均 token 数较低），所以 5/92 ≈ 5.4% 实际命中。

### Token 估法局限
我用的是 `zh_char × 1.5 + en_char × 0.25`（粗糙），与真实 tokenizer 编码可能差 ±20%。要拿准数得拿 MiniCPM-SALA tokenizer 实际跑 1k sample 全部，但 1K sample 已经是 stratified，token 估足够分桶。

### 1K sample 只代表 0.9M 子集
AM-DeepSeek 是 0.5M + 0.9M 两包，stratified sample 来自 0.9M。0.5M 可能分布不同，但量级大致一致。

## 实际怎么验证（不再估）

要拿准数字就 5 分钟脚本：
```python
import zstandard, json, re
zh_re = re.compile(r'[一-鿿]')
n_zh_long_8k = 0
n_zh_long_16k = 0
n_total = 0
for path in ["am_0.5M.jsonl.zst", "am_0.9M.jsonl.zst"]:
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        with zstandard.ZstdDecompressor().stream_reader(f) as r:
            buf = b""
            while chunk := r.read(1<<20):
                buf += chunk; *lines, buf = buf.split(b"\n")
                for ln in lines:
                    n_total += 1
                    r = json.loads(ln)
                    text = "".join(m.get("content","") for m in r.get("messages",[]))
                    if len(zh_re.findall(text)) / max(1,len(text)) < 0.1: continue
                    # tokenize 真实长度
                    n_tok = ...
                    if n_tok >= 8192: n_zh_long_8k += 1
                    if n_tok >= 16384: n_zh_long_16k += 1
print(n_zh_long_8k, n_zh_long_16k, n_total)
```

5.0 GB zst 解压 + 流式读 ≈ 10-15 分钟。要不要跑个 truth？

Based on the search results, I found several relevant resources related to Chinese long context reasoning datasets and models with 32K-64K context capabilities:

## Key Findings:

### Chinese DeepSeek-R1 Distill Dataset
The Chinese-DeepSeek-R1-Distill-data-110k dataset supports 64K context length and 32K output length, with evaluation data including Math, Exam, and other domains that received scoring validation.

### Long-Context Summarization in Chinese
A Chinese novel corpus-based dataset (CNNSum) was built with four subsets at different length levels: L (16k), XL (32k), 2XL (64k), and 3XL (128k), totaling 695 samples. The training dataset contains about 9,000 short novel excerpt summaries with lengths ranging from 2k-4k tokens, with randomly concatenated samples set to lengths of 14k-18k and 30k-34k.

### DeepSeek-R1 Distilled Models
DeepSeek released six distilled variants (1.5B, 7B, 8B, 14B, 32B, and 70B), built on Qwen and Llama base architectures, trained using knowledge distillation from the full R1 model. The DeepSeek-R1 model supports a 64K token context length and can generate maximum 32K tokens for reasoning content, followed by up to 8K tokens for final output.

The search results reveal active development in Chinese language reasoning datasets and distilled models that support the 32K-64K context window specifications you mentioned.

Based on the web search results, here's what I found about DeepSeek-R1 distillation, Chinese SFT datasets, and long context support:

## DeepSeek-R1 Distillation Overview

The reasoning patterns of larger models can be distilled into smaller models, resulting in better performance compared to the reasoning patterns discovered through RL on small models. DeepSeek open-sourced distilled 1.5B, 7B, 8B, 14B, 32B, and 70B checkpoints based on Qwen2.5 and Llama3 series.

## Long Context Support

For all their models, the maximum generation length is set to 32,768 tokens. This means the distilled models support up to 32K context length.

## Chinese SFT Datasets

There are Chinese-language SFT datasets available for DeepSeek-R1:

- The Chinese-DeepSeek-R1-Distill-data-110k dataset contains math data as well as large amounts of general type data, with a total of 110K samples. This dataset is available on both HuggingFace and ModelScope.

- AM-DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-1.4M is an open-source Chinese & English dataset with reasoning traces (1.4 million entries).

These resources and the official DeepSeek-R1 distilled models are available on HuggingFace and ModelScope platforms as of 2025.

Based on the search results, I found information related to your query about long-CoT reasoning datasets and distillation. Here are the key findings:

## Long-CoT Datasets and Distillation

DeepSeek-R1 used reasoning data to fine-tune several dense models, open-sourcing distilled 1.5B, 7B, 8B, 14B, 32B, and 70B checkpoints based on Qwen2.5 and Llama3 series. This is particularly relevant since you mentioned 32K reasoning - the maximum generation length for these models is set to 32,768 tokens.

DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen models were derived from Qwen-2.5 series (originally licensed under Apache 2.0 License) and finetuned with 800k samples curated with DeepSeek-R1.

## Research on Scaling Long-CoT Data

Recent work highlighted that scaling Long-CoT can unlock extraordinary reasoning capabilities, with data and models released at Hugging Face under RedStar-Reasoning.

## Collections on Hugging Face

There are also dedicated collections for long-CoT datasets on Hugging Face, though the full details aren't available in these search results. You may want to visit the collections directly at:
- xianbao's long-cot-dataset collection
- Quadyun's LongCoT Dataset collection

The search results don't show a specific dataset explicitly named "long-cot Chinese dataset 32K" with an Apache 2.0 license from 2026, but the information above covers related distillation work and available models/datasets in this space.

# Chinese-DeepSeek-R1-Distill Dataset Length Analysis

Based on the dataset card information provided, here's what we know about the sample length distribution:

## Token Length Statistics

The dataset includes the following token length fields:

| Metric | Range |
|--------|-------|
| **prompt_tokens_len** | 0 - 2.1k |
| **content_tokens_len** | 1 - 30.6k |
| **reasoning_content_tokens_len** | 5 - 24.7k |

## Key Findings

### Maximum Lengths
- **Longest content output**: ~30.6k tokens
- **Longest reasoning**: ~24.7k tokens
- **Longest prompt/input**: ~2.1k tokens

### Long Samples (≥16K)
**Yes, the dataset includes substantial long samples:**
- Content field goes up to **30.6k tokens**, meaning there are samples exceeding 16K
- Reasoning field reaches **24.7k tokens**, indicating complex chain-of-thought reasoning in many samples

### Average Length
While exact averages aren't explicitly stated, the wide ranges suggest:
- Diverse sample lengths across the 110K dataset
- Mix of short and very long outputs (typical for distilled reasoning datasets)

## Dataset Composition Context

The 110K samples are distributed across:
- **Math**: 36,568 samples (likely longer due to step-by-step reasoning)
- **Exam**: 2,432 samples
- **STEM**: 12,648 samples
- **General**: 58,352 samples (varied lengths)

The presence of extended `reasoning_content` fields (up to 24.7k tokens) aligns with DeepSeek-R1's chain-of-thought distillation approach, which typically produces verbose intermediate reasoning steps before final answers.

Based on the search results, here are some relevant resources for Chinese long-document datasets with 32K+ tokens support:

## Models with 32K+ Context Length

BlueLM is a large-scale pretrained language model developed by vivo AI, which released models supporting 32K long text for both base and chat versions.

ChatGLM-2, based on ChatGLM-6B, extended the base model's context length to 32K and used 8K context length for training during the chat phase.

Qwen (通义千问) models support context lengths of 8-32K and are trained on datasets including text and code covering both general and professional domains.

## Open Source Datasets and Platforms

OpenDataLab released the "书生·万卷" (Shushu Wanjuan) corpus, which contains text datasets sourced from webpages, Wikipedia, professional books, textbooks, and exam questions, with over 500 million documents in text data (approximately half Chinese and half English), totaling over 2TB of data including images and videos.

OpenDataLab from Shanghai AI Laboratory is available as a large-scale open-source Chinese dataset platform, with 5000+ Chinese datasets available for download.

There is a comprehensive open-source project featuring 142 Chinese NLP datasets covering NER, QA, sentiment analysis and other domains, with search functionality available at CLUE Benchmarks.

These resources are available on platforms like HuggingFace and ModelScope for access to both models and datasets optimized for long-context Chinese document understanding.

Based on the web search results, I found relevant information about long-context datasets and Chinese language pretraining resources:

## Long Context Datasets (64K)

Research on scaling long context training data includes document length intervals up to (32K-64K), with each group containing roughly 1 billion tokens. However, there were insufficient documents even from the whole RefinedWeb dataset to construct a (>64K) group, so the research stopped at (32K,64K).

## Chinese Pretraining Datasets (2025 Release)

The OpenCSG Chinese Corpus, released in January 2025, is a series of high-quality datasets specifically designed for LLM pretraining, post-training, and fine-tuning. This corpus includes Fineweb-edu-chinese, Fineweb-edu-chinese-v2, Cosmopedia-chinese, and Smoltalk-chinese, with datasets focusing on filtered high-quality web content, synthetic textbook-style data, and chat-format data respectively.

## Long Data Corpus

A dataset called LongData-Corpus contains samples with length greater than 16k, which can be used for pretraining models with extremely long context lengths, and the dataset is continuously updating.

The search results show active development in both Chinese language datasets and long-context training data in the 2025-2026 timeframe, though I did not find specific results matching all elements of your query (particularly regarding Apache long context datasets).

# LongData-Corpus Dataset Analysis

## Chinese Language Data Subsets

The dataset contains the following Chinese data sources:

1. **悟道200G开源数据** (WuDao 200G Open Source Data)
2. **书生万卷数据集** (OpenDataLab WanJuan 1.0)
3. **CCI中文互联网语料库** (BAAI CCI Chinese Internet Corpus)
4. **中文维基百科** (Chinese Wikipedia)
5. **ShareGPT长对话** (ShareGPT Long Conversations - >8k characters)
6. **中文小说** (Chinese Novels - uploaded to cloud due to large size)

## Language Ratio

The dataset **does not explicitly specify** the Chinese vs English ratio in the provided content. However, it indicates:
- **Chinese data**: >16,000 characters per sample
- **English data**: >16,000 words per sample (from SlimPajama-DC)

## Source Files and Sizes

| Type | Source | Size |
|------|--------|------|
| English | SlimPajama-627B-DC | Part of 36.1 GB total |
| Chinese Novels | Cloud storage (Tsinghua) | Not specified |
| English Books | RedPajamaBook (>100k words) | Part of 36.1 GB total |

**Total Dataset Size**: 36.1 GB

## Length Distribution

- **Primary threshold**: >16,000 characters (Chinese) / >16,000 words (English)
- **ShareGPT conversations**: >8,000 characters
- **English books**: >100,000 words

## License

**cc-by-nc-4.0** (Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial 4.0)

## Data Cleaning Recommendation

```python
import re
# Remove repeated non-alphanumeric characters (4+ times → keep 4)
content = re.sub(r'([^a-zA-Z0-9])\1{4,}', r'\1\1\1\1', content)
# Remove repeated 3+ character substrings
content = re.sub(r'([a-zA-Z0-9]{3,}?)\1+', r'\1', content)
```

**Note**: Access requires accepting data sharing conditions via Hugging Face login.

## ModelScope 上的长中文数据集对比

| 数据集 | 大小 | License | 中文比例 | 内容风格 | 长 ≥16K 估 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`OpenCSG/chinese-cosmopedia`** ⭐ | **70 GB** / 65 parquet × 1.2 GB | **apache-2.0** | 100% | 合成中文**百科 textbook**，长 single-doc | 高 |
| **`OpenCSG/Fineweb-edu-chinese-v2.1`** ⭐ | 18 GB / 100 parquet × 182 MB | apache-2.0 | 100% | **过滤教育 web**，OpenCSG 高质量精选 | 中-高 |
| `OpenCSG/chinese-fineweb-edu-v2` | 94 GB / 100 parquet × 970 MB | apache-2.0 | 100% | 同上 v2 raw | 中-高 |
| `BAAI/CCI3-HQ` | 108 GB | apache-2.0 | 100% | 中文互联网 v3 高质量 | 中 |
| `BAAI/IndustryCorpus2` | 43 GB / 30 行业分类 | apache-2.0 | 100% | 行业预训练（医疗/航空/法律等）| 中 |
| `yuyijiong/LongData-Corpus`（HF）| 36 GB | **cc-by-nc-4.0** | 中英混合 | 含 WanJuan/WuDao/CCI/中文Wiki/**中文小说** native ≥16K chars | **高（专为长 ctx）** |
| `OpenCSG/smoltalk-chinese` | 1.0 GB | apache-2.0 | 100% | 多源中文聊天指令 | 低 |

## 推荐组合（v4 next-iter 用）

**P0 — 主力长 ctx 中文源**：

1. **`OpenCSG/chinese-cosmopedia`**（70 GB，apache-2.0）
   - 是 Cosmopedia (HuggingFaceTB 合成 textbook) 的中文版
   - 单文档天然长（textbook 章节风格），无需 multi-doc pack
   - 质量高（合成 + 过滤），分布与 deepresearch reasoning style 接近
   - 70 GB 太大可只下 5-10 个 parquet（~10 GB）= 几万长样本

2. **`OpenCSG/Fineweb-edu-chinese-v2.1`**（18 GB，apache-2.0）
   - 中文 Fineweb-Edu 精选教育 web
   - 单文档长度多样（教育文章常 1K-30K），质量已过滤
   - 整个 18 GB 可全拉，~ ModelScope 12 MB/s 25 min

**P1 — 长 reasoning 中文**：
- **`yuyijiong/LongData-Corpus`**（36 GB）：HF 上唯一专为长 ctx 设计的中文+英文混合，**全部 ≥16K chars native single-doc**，含 WanJuan/WuDao/中文Wiki/**中文小说/ShareGPT 长对话**。cc-by-nc-4.0（比赛 OK）。需 HF mirror 或镜像源下载（不在 ModelScope 上）。

**保留**：`Chinese-DeepSeek-R1-Distill-110k`（短-中 reasoning，已用本地）

## 关键 trade-off

- cosmopedia/fineweb-edu 是**预训练风格**（不是 reasoning），但单文档长，可解决长 attn 训练信号缺口
- LongData-Corpus 是**真 long native** 中文 + 含 ShareGPT 长对话风格，最对口但 cc-by-nc

Cosmopedia 的合成 textbook 风格 + chinese-fineweb-edu 的教育 web，对长 ctx EAGLE-3 draft 训练**比 R1 reasoning 短样本 multi-doc pack 更靠谱**——LongSpec 论文也是用 SlimPajama (普通 web) + ProLong (long doc) 做长 ctx 训练，不是用 reasoning 数据。

要我下哪个？我建议先拉 **chinese-cosmopedia 5 个 parquet（~6 GB）+ Fineweb-edu-chinese-v2.1 全 18 GB**。

- [Congliu/Chinese-DeepSeek-R1-Distill-data-110k](https://huggingface.co/datasets/Congliu/Chinese-DeepSeek-R1-Distill-data-110k)

- [OpenCSG/chinese-cosmopedia on ModelScope](https://www.modelscope.cn/datasets/OpenCSG/chinese-cosmopedia)

sparse / 改 attention backend\n4. 减小 `EAGLE3_MAX_TOKENS`（当前 65600，长样本可降到 32K + 分段拼接）\n5. 直接用 transformers 跑 forward（不通过 sglang server），精确控制内存\n\n### 4.2 Post-EOS DECODE 不捕获（unk 学不到）\n\n**现象**：现 `demo-sala/sglang/python/sglang/srt/models/minicpm.py:_eagle3_collect` 仅 `ForwardMode.EXTEND` 触发；DECODE 阶段 `_eagle3_aux_cache.clear()` 后 `return`。\n\n**后果**：collect_v4 即便 `max_tokens=50, ignore_eos=True`，target 自延伸的 50 token 不进 .pt 文件。`<unk>` 没有正梯度可学。\n\n**修法**：改 `_eagle3_collect` 让 DECODE 阶段也累积 aux_hidden，每 sample 结束时合并 prefill+decode 段一次性写出。约 50 行 Python 改动。\n\n## 5. 数据 — 用户的关键反馈（**重读**）\n\n### 5.1 我之前推荐预训练数据是错的\n\n之前我推荐 `OpenCSG/chinese-cosmopedia` / `Fineweb-edu-chinese-v2.1` —— **这些是预训练 web/textbook 风格，不是 CoT reasoning**。draft 训练分布必须对齐生产推理（chat-formatted reasoning），用预训练 web 会引入新的训练-推理 mismatch。这条是用户严厉纠正的。\n\n**正确方向**：长中文 CoT / reasoning / chat-formatted 数据，不是预训练 web。\n\n### 5.2 真实 CoT 数据 inventory\n\n| 数据集 | 中文比例 | Max len | ≥16K 样本数 | 风格 | License | 状态 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| `Congliu/Chinese-DeepSeek-R1-Distill-data-110k`（已本地）| 100% | 24,657 | ~41 / 110K | R1 蒸馏 reasoning | apache-2.0 | 已用 |\n| `liucong/...-SFT`（同源） | 100% | 同上 | ~41 / 110K | 同上 SFT 化 | apache-2.0 | 同源不重复 |\n| `a-m-team/AM-DeepSeek-R1-Distilled-1.4M`（已本地 .zst）| 9% Zh | ~17K Zh-long | ~1.4K / 1.4M | R1 蒸馏 reasoning | cc-by-nc-4.0 | **已下未挖** |\n| `OpenO1-SFT` | 估主英文 | 未实测 | 未知 | o1-style CoT | apache-2.0 | 待验中文比例 |\n\n**当前 v4 长 reasoning 中文数据严重不足**：\n- ≥16K 中文 CoT：Chinese-R1-Distill 41 + AM-DeepSeek-Zh ~1400 = 约 1.4K\n- ≥32K 中文 CoT：Chinese-R1-Distill 0 + AM-DeepSeek-Zh ~0 = **几乎无**\n- ≥64K 中文 CoT：**0**\n\n### 5.3 下一轮要找的（明确目标）\n\n**长中文 CoT / reasoning** 候选搜索方向（优先级）：\n\n1. **AM-DeepSeek-R1 1.4M Zh 子集挖掘**（已下未挖）：流式扫 0.5M+0.9M zst，filter `zh_ratio>10% AND tokens≥16K`，估计能拿 ~1.4K 个 ≥16K 中文 long CoT 样本\n2. **AReaL/Light-R1/OpenR1 中文 reasoning 数据**：search ModelScope `qihoo360/Light-R1*` `OpenStellar*` 等，看是否有中文长 CoT\n3. **GLM-Z1 distill / Kimi-distill / QwQ-Plus distill** 中文版：search ModelScope `ZhipuAI` / `moonshotai` / `Qwen` 系下属 reasoning 数据集\n4. **自合成（v5 工作）**：拿 Chinese-R1 短 prompt（user 输入），用 MiniCPM-SALA `temp=0.8, max_tokens=32000` 重生成长 reasoning。这是 SpecForge SpecBundle +23% 的核心机制\n5. **中文长文档 QA（RAG-style）**：长 prompt（document）+ reasoning 短答。production deepresearch 实际是这种 pattern。Search `LongCite` / `LongBench-train` 中文子集 / 中文长文档 QA 数据集\n\n### 5.4 推荐次轮 session 第一步\n\n```bash\n# Step 1: 挖 AM-DeepSeek 1.4M 全量 Chinese × ≥16K\n# 已下载 .zst 在 research/longspec/datasets/，写脚本流式过滤\npython3 -u eagle/pipeline/am_deepseek_filter.py \\\n    --in_zst research/longspec/datasets/.../am_0.5M.jsonl.zst,research/longspec/datasets/.../am_0.9M.jsonl.zst \\\n    --min_zh_ratio 0.10 --min_tokens 16384 \\\n    --out /tmp/am_zh_long.jsonl\n\n# Step 2: 单独跑长 ctx CoT search\npython3 modelscope_search_long_cot.py  # 待写\n```\n\n## 6. v4 next-iter 执行清单\n\n按依赖顺序：\n\n| # | 项 | 阻塞？ |\n|---|---|---|\n| 1 | 解 server 长 shard OOM（4.1）| 是，阻塞 6/7 |\n| 2 | 改
